    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."

    def test_r_loaddlg_20_3_right_click_path_box_refuses_unsafe(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

    def query_one(
        self,
        selector: str | type[QueryType],
        expect_type: type[QueryType] | None = None,
    ) -> QueryType | Widget:
        """Get a widget from this widget's children that matches a selector or widget type.
    
        Args:
            selector: A selector or widget type.
            expect_type: Require the object be of the supplied type, or None for any type.
    
        Raises:
            WrongType: If the wrong type was found.
            NoMatches: If no node matches the query.
    
        Returns:
            A widget matching the selector.
        """
        _rich_traceback_omit = True
    
        base_node = self._get_dom_base()
    
        if isinstance(selector, str):
            query_selector = selector
        else:
            query_selector = selector.__name__
    
        if is_id_selector(query_selector):
            cache_key = (base_node._nodes._updates, query_selector, expect_type)
            cached_result = base_node._query_one_cache.get(cache_key)
            if cached_result is not None:
                return cached_result
            if (
                node := walk_breadth_search_id(
                    base_node, query_selector[1:], with_root=False
                )
            ) is not None:
                if expect_type is not None and not isinstance(node, expect_type):
                    raise WrongType(
                        f"Node matching {query_selector!r} is the wrong type; expected type {expect_type.__name__!r}, found {node}"
                    )
                base_node._query_one_cache[cache_key] = node
                return node

    def test_r_paste_5_no_selection_changes_no_clipboard(tmp_path: Path, action: str) -> None:
        """R-PASTE.5/1-3 (LinuxH): no selection → the app clipboard keeps BEFORE."""
        _assert_linuxh_headless()
    
        async def _drive() -> str:

                await pilot.pause()
                if action == "x_multi":
                    app.action_show_screen("patch")
                    for _ in range(4):
                        await pilot.pause()
                    widget = app.query_one("#patch_paste_text", TextArea)
                    widget.text = "KEEP"
                    widget.selection = ((0, 4), (0, 4))
                    widget.focus()
                    await pilot.pause()
                    await pilot.press("ctrl+x")
                else:

                    for _ in range(4):
                        await pilot.pause()

                    widget.value = "KEEP"
                    widget.selection = (4, 4)
                    widget.focus()
                    await pilot.pause()
                    await pilot.press(action)
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.pause()
                return app.clipboard

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def test_r_tui_043_6_5_right_click_no_selection_pastes_app_clipboard(
        tmp_path: Path,

        async def _drive() -> tuple:

                box_m.focus()
                await pilot.pause()
                await right_click_app_route(app, box_m)
                await pilot.pause()
                return a_value, box_m.text

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."

    def test_r_tui_043_12_3_warning_line_on_right_click_no_text(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        """R-TUI-043.12/3 (LinuxH): ≥ 1 WARNING of os_clipboard_input per right-click."""
        _assert_linuxh_headless()
    
        async def _drive() -> int:
            app = S19TuiApp(base_dir=tmp_path)
            app._clipboard = "APP-TEXT"
            async with app.run_test(size=(160, 48)) as pilot:
                await pilot.pause()

                app.action_show_screen("a2l")
                for _ in range(4):
                    await pilot.pause()
                box = app.query_one("#a2l_tags_filter_input", Input)
                box.focus()
                await pilot.pause()

                await pilot.pause()
                warnings = [
                    r for r in records
                    if r[0] == "s19_app.tui.os_clipboard_input" and r[1] == "WARNING"
                ]
                remove_log_capture(records)
                return len(warnings)

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."

    def test_r_paste_27_3_linux_right_click_copy_ends_within_typed_key_time(

                    before = len(notices)
                    start = time.monotonic()
                    await right_click_app_route(app, box)
                    deadline = time.monotonic() + 10.0
                    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                        await asyncio.sleep(0.005)
                        await pilot.pause()
                        if len(notices) > before:
                            break
                    samples.append(time.monotonic() - start)
                samples.sort()
                return key_time, samples[len(samples) // 2]

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def mount(
        self,
        *widgets: Widget,
        before: int | str | Widget | None = None,
        after: int | str | Widget | None = None,
    ) -> AwaitMount:
        """Mount widgets below this widget (making this widget a container).
    
        Args:
            *widgets: The widget(s) to mount.
            before: Optional location to mount before. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount before, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount before.
            after: Optional location to mount after. An `int` is the index
                of the child to mount after, a `str` is a `query_one` query to
                find the widget to mount after.
    
        Returns:
            An awaitable object that waits for widgets to be mounted.
    
        Raises:
            MountError: If there is a problem with the mount request.
    
        Note:
            Only one of ``before`` or ``after`` can be provided. If both are
            provided a ``MountError`` will be raised.
        """
        if self._closing or self._pruning:
            return AwaitMount(self, [])
        if not self.is_attached:
            raise MountError(f"Can't mount widget(s) before {self!r} is mounted")
        # Check for duplicate IDs in the incoming widgets
        ids_to_mount = [
            widget_id for widget in widgets if (widget_id := widget.id) is not None
        ]
        if len(set(ids_to_mount)) != len(ids_to_mount):
            counter = Counter(ids_to_mount)
            for widget_id, count in counter.items():
                if count > 1:
>                   raise MountError(
                        f"Tried to insert {count!r} widgets with the same ID {widget_id!r}. "
                        "Widget IDs must be unique."
                    )

    def require_isolated_clipboard(
        kind: str,
        environ: Optional[dict] = None,
        platform: Optional[str] = None,
        account: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Fail the test (never skip) before anything opens a clipboard.
    
        Summary:
            The isolation guard of this module's helpers: calls
            :func:`clipboard_isolated` and ``pytest.fail``s with
            ``OS clipboard not isolated: <reason>`` when it is not isolated. It
            opens nothing itself; every clipboard helper of this module calls it
            first.
    
        Args:
            kind (str): ``"windows"`` or ``"x"``.
            environ (Optional[dict]): Passed through to :func:`clipboard_isolated`.
            platform (Optional[str]): Passed through.
            account (Optional[str]): Passed through.
        """
        ok, reason = clipboard_isolated(kind, environ, platform, account)
        if not ok:

    def test_r_tui_044_2_8_win32_read_bounded_by_global_size(monkeypatch) -> None:

tests/test_b103_clipboard_core.py:2302: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

    def _read_via_ctypes() -> Optional[str]:
        """Layer 1 — Win32 clipboard API through ``ctypes`` with short retries.
    
        Uses ``OpenClipboard(NULL)`` so no window handle is needed. Bypasses
        the Tcl interpreter and any of its window-manager expectations. The
        read is bounded by ``GlobalSize`` so an unterminated UTF-16 block
        cannot read past the clipboard block. Returns ``None`` on any
        non-Windows platform or when it could not answer (the clipboard is
        held, ``GlobalLock`` fails, or the size is 0); returns ``''`` when the
        clipboard opened but holds no ``CF_UNICODETEXT`` (a file-drop list or
        an image) or an empty text — answered, no text.
        """
        if sys.platform != "win32":
            return None
        try:
            import ctypes
            from ctypes import wintypes
        except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover — ctypes ships with CPython.
            logger.debug("_read_via_ctypes: ctypes unavailable: %s", exc)
            return None
    
        user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
        kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32

_________ test_at_i1a_matrix_copy_then_paste[M->A_right_click_ctrl_v] __________

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-<user>/pytest-1002/test_at_i1a_matrix_copy_then_p13')
cell = {'copy': 'right_click', 'route': 'ctrl_v', 'source': ('patch', '#patch_paste_text', 'textarea'), 'target': ('diff', '#diff_path_a', 'input')}

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("cell", _MATRIX_CELLS, ids=[_cell_id(c) for c in _MATRIX_CELLS])
    def test_at_i1a_matrix_copy_then_paste(tmp_path: Path, cell: dict) -> None:
        """One matrix cell (AT-I1a-1 … 8, 12 … 27, LinuxH arm): copy from the
        source by C1/C2, paste into the target by route 1/2/3, assert the text."""
        if not _LINUXH:
            pytest.skip("Windows only")
    
        async def _drive() -> str:
            async for app, pilot in _app_with(tmp_path):
                osc = install_osc52_spy(app)
                source = await _source_widget(app, pilot, cell["source"])
                payload = _payload_for(cell["source"])
                _set_box(source, payload)
                await _select(source)
                source.focus()
                await pilot.pause()
                # Copy
                if cell["copy"] == "ctrl_c":
                    await pilot.press("ctrl+c")
                else:
                    await right_click_app_route(app, source)
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.pause()
                # Target
                target = await _target_widget(app, pilot, cell["target"])
                await _clear(target)
                target.focus()
                await pilot.pause()
                if cell["route"] == "ctrl_v":
                    await pilot.press("ctrl+v")
                elif cell["route"] == "terminal":
                    if osc:
                        await terminal_paste(app, osc[-1])
                    else:
                        await terminal_paste(app, app.clipboard)
                else:
                    await right_click_app_route(app, target)
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.pause()
                return _box(target)
    
        result = asyncio.run(_drive())
        payload = _payload_for(cell["source"])
        expected = _expected_for(cell["source"], cell["target"], payload)
>       assert result == expected, (cell["source"], cell["target"], cell["copy"], cell["route"], result)

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-<user>/pytest-1002/test_r_tui_044_1_walk_single_l0')
place = ('workspace', '#search_input', False)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "place", _single_line_places(), ids=[p[0] for p in _single_line_places()]
    )
    def test_r_tui_044_1_walk_single_line_round_trip(tmp_path: Path, place: tuple) -> None:
        """R-TUI-044.1/1-6,9 (LinuxH): a Ctrl+C/Ctrl+V and a right-click round trip."""
        if not _LINUXH:
            pytest.skip("Windows only")
        screen_key, widget_id, _ = place
    
        async def _drive() -> tuple:
            async for app, pilot in _app_with(tmp_path):
                await _activate(app, pilot, screen_key)
                widget = app.query_one(widget_id, Input)
                widget.focus()
                await pilot.pause()
                # Ctrl round trip
                widget.value = f"W1-{P1}"
                widget.selection = (0, len(widget.value))
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.press("ctrl+c")
                await pilot.pause()
                copied = app.clipboard
                widget.value = ""
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.press("ctrl+v")
                await pilot.pause()
                ctrl_value = widget.value
                # Right-click round trip
                widget.value = f"R1-{P1}"
                widget.selection = (0, len(widget.value))
                await pilot.pause()
                await right_click_app_route(app, widget)
                await pilot.pause()
                rc_copied = app.clipboard
                widget.value = ""
                await pilot.pause()
                await right_click_app_route(app, widget)
                await pilot.pause()
                return copied, ctrl_value, rc_copied, widget.value
    
        copied, ctrl_value, rc_copied, rc_value = asyncio.run(_drive())
        assert copied == f"W1-{P1}", copied
        assert ctrl_value == f"W1-{P1}", ctrl_value

______________ test_r_tui_044_1_walk_single_line_round_trip[mac] _______________

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-<user>/pytest-1002/test_r_tui_044_1_walk_single_l2')
place = ('mac', '#mac_search_input', False)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "place", _single_line_places(), ids=[p[0] for p in _single_line_places()]
    )
    def test_r_tui_044_1_walk_single_line_round_trip(tmp_path: Path, place: tuple) -> None:
        """R-TUI-044.1/1-6,9 (LinuxH): a Ctrl+C/Ctrl+V and a right-click round trip."""
        if not _LINUXH:
            pytest.skip("Windows only")
        screen_key, widget_id, _ = place
    
        async def _drive() -> tuple:
            async for app, pilot in _app_with(tmp_path):
                await _activate(app, pilot, screen_key)
                widget = app.query_one(widget_id, Input)
                widget.focus()
                await pilot.pause()
                # Ctrl round trip
                widget.value = f"W1-{P1}"
                widget.selection = (0, len(widget.value))
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.press("ctrl+c")
                await pilot.pause()
                copied = app.clipboard
                widget.value = ""
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.press("ctrl+v")
                await pilot.pause()
                ctrl_value = widget.value
                # Right-click round trip
                widget.value = f"R1-{P1}"
                widget.selection = (0, len(widget.value))
                await pilot.pause()
                await right_click_app_route(app, widget)
                await pilot.pause()
                rc_copied = app.clipboard
                widget.value = ""
                await pilot.pause()
                await right_click_app_route(app, widget)
                await pilot.pause()
                return copied, ctrl_value, rc_copied, widget.value
    
        copied, ctrl_value, rc_copied, rc_value = asyncio.run(_drive())
        assert copied == f"W1-{P1}", copied
        assert ctrl_value == f"W1-{P1}", ctrl_value
        assert rc_copied == f"R1-{P1}", rc_copied
>       assert rc_value == f"R1-{P1}", rc_value

E       assert '' == 'R1-COPY-1 Ñoño Ж 漢字 😀'

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-<user>/pytest-1002/test_r_tui_044_1_walk_single_l5')
place = ('crc_designer', '#crc_field_width', False)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "place", _single_line_places(), ids=[p[0] for p in _single_line_places()]
    )
    def test_r_tui_044_1_walk_single_line_round_trip(tmp_path: Path, place: tuple) -> None:
        """R-TUI-044.1/1-6,9 (LinuxH): a Ctrl+C/Ctrl+V and a right-click round trip."""
        if not _LINUXH:
            pytest.skip("Windows only")
        screen_key, widget_id, _ = place
    
        async def _drive() -> tuple:
            async for app, pilot in _app_with(tmp_path):
                await _activate(app, pilot, screen_key)
                widget = app.query_one(widget_id, Input)
                widget.focus()
                await pilot.pause()
                # Ctrl round trip
                widget.value = f"W1-{P1}"
                widget.selection = (0, len(widget.value))
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.press("ctrl+c")
                await pilot.pause()
                copied = app.clipboard
                widget.value = ""
                await pilot.pause()
                await pilot.press("ctrl+v")
                await pilot.pause()
                ctrl_value = widget.value
                # Right-click round trip
                widget.value = f"R1-{P1}"
                widget.selection = (0, len(widget.value))
                await pilot.pause()
                await right_click_app_route(app, widget)
                await pilot.pause()
                rc_copied = app.clipboard
                widget.value = ""
                await pilot.pause()
                await right_click_app_route(app, widget)
                await pilot.pause()
                return copied, ctrl_value, rc_copied, widget.value
    
        copied, ctrl_value, rc_copied, rc_value = asyncio.run(_drive())
        assert copied == f"W1-{P1}", copied
        assert ctrl_value == f"W1-{P1}", ctrl_value
        assert rc_copied == f"R1-{P1}", rc_copied
